     1 # Supabase Frontend — Web (Vite + React)
     2 
     3 ## Environment Variables
     4 
     5 - Prefix: `VITE_`
     6 - Access: `import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL` / `import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY`
     7 - Written automatically into `.env` after `supabase_init`
     8 
     9 ## Type Definitions
    10 
    11 - Define types in `@/types/types.ts` matching SQL schema
    12 - When unsure whether a field exists, query the table structure first
    13 
    14 ## api.ts Coding Standards
    15 
    16 - Use `.maybeSingle()` instead of `.single()`
    17 - Always use `.order()` with `.limit()`
    18 - When writing select queries, avoid `table_name(*)` — use `table_name!foreign_key_name` to explicitly specify relationships and prevent ambiguity
    19 - Implement pagination for multiple results — **strictly prohibit** fetching all data without pagination
    20 - **Prefer cursor-based pagination**
    21 - Return arrays safely: `Array.isArray(data) ? data : []`
    22 - Empty strings need to be converted to NULL to prevent SQL formatting misalignment
    23 - Prefer `.insert()` without `.select()`
    24 - Protect nulls: `meeting.participants?.length`, `meeting.title || 'Untitled'`
    25 - Use Supabase Realtime for real-time data updates instead of polling
    26 
    27 ## Storage Upload (Frontend)
    28 
    29 - Create for image/file uploads only
    30 - **NEVER** store images/videos as Base64 in database
    31 - Frontend validation: 1MB limit, snake_case filenames
    32 - Web: upload directly using `File` / `Blob`:
    33 
    34 ```ts
    35 const { data, error } = await supabase.storage
    36   .from(bucket)
    37   .upload(`${userId}/${snakeCaseName}.jpg`, file, { contentType: file.type });
    38 
    39 const { data: urlData } = supabase.storage.from(bucket).getPublicUrl(data.path);
    40 // Store urlData.publicUrl in database
    41 ```
    42 
    43 ## Edge Function Invocation
    44 
    45 - Always use `supabase.functions.invoke`, except when a non-standard or custom Content-Type is required
    46 - Read `error.context.text()` for the real error message
    47 - For GET requests with parameters, append query parameters directly to the function name: `supabase.functions.invoke('test-fn?id=123', { method: 'GET' })`
    48 
    49 ```ts
    50 // Type signature
    51 supabase.functions.invoke<T = any>(
    52   functionName: string,
    53   options?: {
    54     body?: any
    55     headers?: Record<string, string>
    56     method?: 'GET' | 'POST' | 'PUT' | 'PATCH' | 'DELETE'
    57     signal?: AbortSignal
    58   }
    59 ): Promise<{
    60   data: T | null
    61   error: FunctionsHttpError | null // Read error.context.text() for real message
    62 }>
    63 
    64 const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke('hello_world', {
    65   body: { key: 'value' },
    66   method: 'POST',
    67 });
    68 
    69 if (error) {
    70   const errorMsg = await error?.context?.text();
    71   console.error("edge function error in <hello_world>:", errorMsg || error?.message);
    72 }
    73 ```
    74 
    75 ## Realtime Subscription (low-frequency feature)
    76 
    77 Use only when realtime capability is genuinely needed. Full spec (server-side publication switch, subscription template, per-stack differences) is in `supabase-server/references/realtime.md`.
    78 
    79 ## Auth
    80 
    81 For login/signup/OAuth/SSO implementation, follow the `login` skill — **MUST** call `skill_action(skill="login")` to get the latest spec.

   218     .from('sales_plans')
   219     .select('plan_amount')
   220     .eq('month_year', monthYear);

     1 import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
     2 // @ts-ignore
     3 import { supabase } from '@/db/supabase';
     4 import type { User } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
     5 // @ts-ignore
     6 import type { Profile } from '@/types/types';
     7 import { toast } from 'sonner';
     8 
     9 export async function getProfile(userId: string): Promise<Profile | null> {
    10   const { data, error } = await supabase
    11     .from('profiles')
    12     .select('*')
    13     .eq('id', userId)
    14     .maybeSingle();
    15 
    16   if (error) {
    17     console.error('获取用户信息失败:', error);
    18     return null;
    19   }
    20   return data;
    21 }
    22 interface AuthContextType {
    23   user: User | null;
    24   profile: Profile | null;
    25   loading: boolean;
    26   signInWithUsername: (username: string, password: string) => Promise<{ error: Error | null }>;
    27   signUpWithUsername: (username: string, password: string) => Promise<{ error: Error | null }>;
    28   signOut: () => Promise<void>;
    29   refreshProfile: () => Promise<void>;
    30 }
    31 
    32 const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextType | undefined>(undefined);
    33 
    34 export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
    35   const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
    36   const [profile, setProfile] = useState<Profile | null>(null);
    37   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    38 
    39   const refreshProfile = async () => {
    40     if (!user) {
    41       setProfile(null);
    42       return;
    43     }
    44 
    45     const profileData = await getProfile(user.id);
    46     setProfile(profileData);
    47   };
    48 
    49   useEffect(() => {
    50     supabase
    51       .auth
    52       .getSession()
    53       // @ts-ignore
    54       .then(({ data: { session } }) => {
    55         setUser(session?.user ?? null);
    56         if (session?.user) {
    57           getProfile(session.user.id).then(setProfile);
    58         }
    59       })
    60       // @ts-ignore
    61       .catch(error => {
    62         toast.error(`获取用户信息失败: ${error.message}`);
    63       })
    64       .finally(() => {
    65         setLoading(false);
    66       });
    67 
    68     // @ts-ignore
    69     // In this function, do NOT use any await calls. Use `.then()` instead to avoid deadlocks.
    70     const { data: { subscription } } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_event, session) => {
    71       setUser(session?.user ?? null);
    72       if (session?.user) {
    73         getProfile(session.user.id).then(setProfile);
    74       } else {
    75         setProfile(null);
    76       }
    77     });
    78 
    79     return () => subscription.unsubscribe();
    80   }, []);
    81 
    82   const signInWithUsername = async (username: string, password: string) => {
    83     try {
    84       const email = `${username}@miaoda.com`;
    85       const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({
    86         email,
    87         password,
    88       });
    89 
    90       if (error) throw error;
    91       return { error: null };
    92     } catch (error) {
    93       return { error: error as Error };
    94     }
    95   };
    96 
    97   const signUpWithUsername = async (username: string, password: string) => {
    98     try {
    99       const email = `${username}@miaoda.com`;
   100       const { error } = await supabase.auth.signUp({
   101         email,
   102         password,
   103       });
   104 
   105       if (error) throw error;
   106       return { error: null };
   107     } catch (error) {
   108       return { error: error as Error };
   109     }
   110   };
   111 
   112   const signOut = async () => {
   113     await supabase.auth.signOut();
   114     setUser(null);
   115     setProfile(null);
   116   };
   117 
   118   return (
   119     <AuthContext.Provider value={{ user, profile, loading, signInWithUsername, signUpWithUsername, signOut, refreshProfile }}>
   120       {children}
   121     </AuthContext.Provider>
   122   );
   123 }
   124 
   125 export function useAuth() {
   126   const context = useContext(AuthContext);
   127   if (context === undefined) {
   128     throw new Error('useAuth must be used within an AuthProvider');
   129   }
   130   return context;
   131 }

    10 
    11 export default function SalesPage() {
    12   const [monthYear, setMonthYear] = useState(getCurrentMonthYear());
    13   const [managers, setManagers] = useState<Manager[]>([]);
    14   const [deals, setDeals] = useState<Record<string, Deal[]>>({});
    15   const [plans, setPlans] = useState<SalesPlan[]>([]);
    16   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    17   const planSaveTimers = useRef<Record<string, ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>>({});
    18 
    19   const loadData = useCallback(async () => {
    20     setLoading(true);
    21     try {
    22       const mgrs = await getManagers();
    23       setManagers(mgrs);
    24       const [allPlans, ...dealsByManager] = await Promise.all([
    25         getSalesPlans(monthYear),
    26         ...mgrs.map(m => getDeals(m.id, monthYear)),
    27       ]);
    28       setPlans(allPlans as SalesPlan[]);
    29       const dealsMap: Record<string, Deal[]> = {};
    30       mgrs.forEach((m, i) => { dealsMap[m.id] = dealsByManager[i] as Deal[]; });
    31       setDeals(dealsMap);
    32     } catch {
    33       // silent
    34     } finally {
    35       setLoading(false);
    36     }
    37   }, [monthYear]);
    38 
    39   useEffect(() => { loadData(); }, [loadData]);
    40 
    41   function getPlan(managerId: string): SalesPlan | undefined {
    42     return plans.find(p => p.manager_id === managerId && p.month_year === monthYear);
    43   }
    44 
    45   function handlePlanChange(managerId: string, field: 'plan_amount' | 'net_profit_plan' | 'dividends_plan', value: number) {
    46     setPlans(prev => {
    47       const existing = prev.find(p => p.manager_id === managerId && p.month_year === monthYear);
    48       if (existing) {
    49         return prev.map(p =>
    50           p.manager_id === managerId && p.month_year === monthYear
    51             ? { ...p, [field]: value }
    52             : p
    53         );
    54       }
    55       return [...prev, {
    56         id: `temp-${managerId}`,
    57         manager_id: managerId,
    58         month_year: monthYear,
    59         plan_amount: 0,
    60         net_profit_plan: null,
    61         dividends_plan: null,
    62         created_at: '',
    63         updated_at: '',
    64         [field]: value,
    65       }];
    66     });
    67 
    68     // Debounced save
    69     const key = `${managerId}:${monthYear}`;
    70     clearTimeout(planSaveTimers.current[key]);
    71     planSaveTimers.current[key] = setTimeout(async () => {
    72       const currentPlan = plans.find(p => p.manager_id === managerId && p.month_year === monthYear);
    73       const updated = { ...(currentPlan || { plan_amount: 0, net_profit_plan: null, dividends_plan: null }), [field]: value };
    74       await upsertSalesPlan({
    75         manager_id: managerId,
    76         month_year: monthYear,
    77         plan_amount: Number(updated.plan_amount) || 0,
    78         net_profit_plan: updated.net_profit_plan != null ? Number(updated.net_profit_plan) : null,
    79         dividends_plan: updated.dividends_plan != null ? Number(updated.dividends_plan) : null,
    80       });
    81     }, 800);
    82   }
    83 
    84   const months = getAvailableMonths();
    85 
    86   return (
    87     <AppLayout>
    88       <div className="space-y-6">
    89         {/* Header */}
    90         <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between gap-3">
    91           <div>
    92             <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold">Продажи</h1>
    93             <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground mt-0.5">Учёт сделок по менеджерам</p>
    94           </div>
    95           <Select value={monthYear} onValueChange={setMonthYear}>
    96             <SelectTrigger className="w-52">
    97               <SelectValue />
    98             </SelectTrigger>
    99             <SelectContent>
   100               {months.map(m => (
   101                 <SelectItem key={m} value={m}>{monthYearToLabel(m)}</SelectItem>
   102               ))}
   103             </SelectContent>
   104           </Select>
   105         </div>
   106 
   107         {loading ? (
   108           <div className="space-y-4">
   109             {[1, 2, 3].map(i => (
   110               <div key={i} className="h-32 rounded-lg bg-muted animate-pulse" />
   111             ))}
   112           </div>
   113         ) : managers.length === 0 ? (
   114           <div className="text-center py-16 text-muted-foreground">
   115             <p>Нет менеджеров.</p>
   116             <p className="text-sm mt-1">Перейдите в раздел «Менеджеры» и добавьте первого.</p>
   117           </div>
   118         ) : (
   119           <div className="space-y-4">
   120             {managers.map(m => (
   121               <ManagerSalesSection
   122                 key={m.id}
   123                 manager={m}
   124                 monthYear={monthYear}
   125                 deals={deals[m.id] || []}
   126                 plan={getPlan(m.id)}
   127                 onPlanChange={handlePlanChange}
   128                 onRefresh={loadData}
   129               />
   130             ))}
   131           </div>
   132         )}
   133       </div>
   134     </AppLayout>
   135   );
   136 }

    34 // ─── helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    35 function inRange(dateStr: string, from: string, to: string): boolean {
    36   if (!from && !to) return true;
    37   if (from && dateStr < from) return false;
    38   if (to && dateStr > to) return false;
    39   return true;
    40 }
    41 
    42 // ─── Balance summary (transfers cancel out per channel) ────────────────────────
    43 function BalanceSummary({ expenses, income, transfers }: {
    44   expenses: Expense[]; income: Income[]; transfers: Transfer[];
    45 }) {
    46   const channels: string[] = ['Каспи', 'Халык', 'Фридом', 'Наличные'];
    47   return (
    48     <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
    49       {channels.map(ch => {
    50         const inc = income.filter(i => i.channel === ch).reduce((s, i) => s + Number(i.total_amount), 0);
    51         const exp = expenses.filter(e => e.channel === ch).reduce((s, e) => s + Number(e.amount), 0);
    52         // transfers: outgoing reduces balance, incoming increases — net effect zero across all channels
    53         const trOut = transfers.filter(t => t.from_channel === ch).reduce((s, t) => s + Number(t.amount), 0);
    54         const trIn  = transfers.filter(t => t.to_channel   === ch).reduce((s, t) => s + Number(t.amount), 0);
    55         const balance = inc - exp - trOut + trIn;
    56         return (
    57           <div key={ch} className="rounded-md border border-border p-3">
    58             <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{ch}</p>
    59             <p className={`text-base font-semibold mt-1 ${balance < 0 ? 'text-destructive' : 'text-foreground'}`}>
    60               {formatCurrency(balance)}

}

export async function createProfile(p: Omit<Profile, 'created_at' | 'updated_at'>): Promise<void> {

export async function createManager(name: string, role = 'Менеджер по продажам', userId?: string | null): Promise<string> {
  const payload: Record<string, string | null> = { name: name.trim(), role };
  if (userId) payload.user_id = userId;
  const { data, error } = await supabase.from('managers').insert(payload).select('id').maybeSingle();
  if (error) throw error;
  return data?.id ?? '';
}

export async function updateManager(id: string, name: string, role?: string, userId?: string | null): Promise<void> {
  const updates: Record<string, string | null> = { name: name.trim() };
  if (role !== undefined) updates.role = role;
  if (userId !== undefined) updates.user_id = userId;
  const { error } = await supabase.from('managers').update(updates).eq('id', id);
  if (error) throw error;
}

export async function approveDeal(id: string): Promise<void> {
  const { error } = await supabase.from('deals').update({ status: 'approved', updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('id', id);
  if (error) throw error;

export async function rejectDeal(id: string): Promise<void> {
  const { error } = await supabase.from('deals').update({ status: 'rejected', updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('id', id);
  if (error) throw error;
}

export async function getPendingDeals(): Promise<(Deal & { manager_name?: string })[]> {
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('deals')
    .select('*')
    .eq('status', 'pending')
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    .limit(200);
  if (error) throw error;
  return Array.isArray(data) ? data : [];
}

import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
// @ts-ignore
import { supabase } from '@/db/supabase';
import type { User } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
// @ts-ignore
import type { Profile } from '@/types/types';
import { toast } from 'sonner';

export async function getProfile(userId: string): Promise<Profile | null> {
  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('profiles')
    .select('*')
    .eq('id', userId)
    .maybeSingle();

  if (error) {
    console.error('获取用户信息失败:', error);
    return null;
  }
  return data;
}
interface AuthContextType {
  user: User | null;
  profile: Profile | null;
  loading: boolean;
  signInWithUsername: (username: string, password: string) => Promise<{ error: Error | null }>;
  signUpWithUsername: (username: string, password: string) => Promise<{ error: Error | null }>;
  signOut: () => Promise<void>;
  refreshProfile: () => Promise<void>;
}

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextType | undefined>(undefined);

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState<Profile | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const refreshProfile = async () => {
    if (!user) {
      setProfile(null);
      return;
    }

    const profileData = await getProfile(user.id);
    setProfile(profileData);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    supabase
      .auth
      .getSession()
      // @ts-ignore
      .then(({ data: { session } }) => {
        setUser(session?.user ?? null);
        if (session?.user) {
          getProfile(session.user.id).then(setProfile);
        }
      })
      // @ts-ignore
      .catch(error => {
        toast.error(`获取用户信息失败: ${error.message}`);
      })
      .finally(() => {
        setLoading(false);
      });

    // @ts-ignore
    // In this function, do NOT use any await calls. Use `.then()` instead to avoid deadlocks.
    const { data: { subscription } } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_event, session) => {
      setUser(session?.user ?? null);
      if (session?.user) {
        getProfile(session.user.id).then(setProfile);
      } else {
        setProfile(null);
      }
    });

    return () => subscription.unsubscribe();
  }, []);

  const signInWithUsername = async (username: string, password: string) => {
    try {
      const email = `${username}@miaoda.com`;
      const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({
        email,
        password,
      });

      if (error) throw error;
      return { error: null };
    } catch (error) {
      return { error: error as Error };
    }
  };

  const signUpWithUsername = async (username: string, password: string) => {
    try {
      const email = `${username}@miaoda.com`;
      const { error } = await supabase.auth.signUp({
        email,
        password,
      });

      if (error) throw error;
      return { error: null };
    } catch (error) {
      return { error: error as Error };
    }
  };

  const signOut = async () => {
    await supabase.auth.signOut();
    setUser(null);
    setProfile(null);
  };

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider value={{ user, profile, loading, signInWithUsername, signUpWithUsername, signOut, refreshProfile }}>
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useAuth() {
  const context = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (context === undefined) {
    throw new Error('useAuth must be used within an AuthProvider');
  }
  return context;
}
```
New String: ```
import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { supabase } from '@/db/supabase';
import type { User } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import type { Profile } from '@/types/types';

async function fetchProfile(userId: string): Promise<Profile | null> {
  const { data } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('*').eq('id', userId).maybeSingle();
  return data ?? null;
}

interface AuthContextType {
  user: User | null;
  profile: Profile | null;
  isAdmin: boolean;
  loading: boolean;
  signIn: (username: string, password: string) => Promise<{ error: string | null }>;
  signOut: () => Promise<void>;
  refreshProfile: () => Promise<void>;
}

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextType | undefined>(undefined);

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [user, setUser]       = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState<Profile | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const isAdmin = profile?.role === 'admin';

  const refreshProfile = async () => {
    if (!user) { setProfile(null); return; }
    const p = await fetchProfile(user.id);
    setProfile(p);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    supabase.auth.getSession().then(({ data: { session } }) => {
      setUser(session?.user ?? null);
      if (session?.user) fetchProfile(session.user.id).then(setProfile);
    }).finally(() => setLoading(false));

    const { data: { subscription } } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_e, session) => {
      setUser(session?.user ?? null);
      if (session?.user) fetchProfile(session.user.id).then(setProfile);
      else setProfile(null);
    });

    return () => subscription.unsubscribe();
  }, []);

  const signIn = async (username: string, password: string) => {
    const email = `${username.trim().toLowerCase()}@aybar.app`;
    const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({ email, password });
    if (error) return { error: 'Неверный логин или пароль' };
    return { error: null };
  };

  const signOut = async () => {
    await supabase.auth.signOut();
    setUser(null);
    setProfile(null);
  };

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider value={{ user, profile, isAdmin, loading, signIn, signOut, refreshProfile }}>
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useAuth() {
  const ctx = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useAuth must be used within AuthProvider');
  return ctx;
}

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '@/contexts/AuthContext';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { LogIn } from 'lucide-react';

export default function LoginPage() {
  const { signIn } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [username, setUsername] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  async function handleSubmit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!username.trim() || !password) { toast.error('Введите логин и пароль'); return; }
    setLoading(true);
    const { error } = await signIn(username, password);
    setLoading(false);
    if (error) { toast.error(error); return; }
    navigate('/', { replace: true });
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-background px-4">
      <div className="w-full max-w-sm space-y-8">
        <div className="text-center space-y-1">
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Aybar Finance</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Войдите в свой аккаунт</p>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label htmlFor="username">Логин</Label>
            <Input
              id="username"
              autoComplete="username"
              placeholder="Ваш логин"
              value={username}
              onChange={e => setUsername(e.target.value)}
              disabled={loading}
            />
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label htmlFor="password">Пароль</Label>
            <Input
              id="password"
              type="password"
              autoComplete="current-password"
              placeholder="••••••••"
              value={password}
              onChange={e => setPassword(e.target.value)}
              disabled={loading}
            />
          </div>
          <Button type="submit" className="w-full" disabled={loading}>
            {loading ? 'Вход...' : (
              <><LogIn size={16} className="mr-2" />Войти</>
            )}
          </Button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

```
import React from 'react';
import { Navigate, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '@/contexts/AuthContext';

interface RouteGuardProps {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  adminOnly?: boolean;
}

export function RouteGuard({ children, adminOnly = false }: RouteGuardProps) {
  const { user, profile, loading } = useAuth();
  const location = useLocation();

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-background">
        <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full border-2 border-primary border-t-transparent animate-spin" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!user) {
    return <Navigate to="/login" state={{ from: location }} replace />;
  }

  if (adminOnly && profile?.role !== 'admin') {
    return <Navigate to="/" replace />;
  }

  return <>{children}</>;
}

```
import React, { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import AppLayout from '@/components/layouts/AppLayout';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import {
  AlertDialog, AlertDialogAction, AlertDialogCancel,
  AlertDialogContent, AlertDialogDescription, AlertDialogFooter,
  AlertDialogHeader, AlertDialogTitle, AlertDialogTrigger,
} from '@/components/ui/alert-dialog';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { CheckCircle, XCircle, Clock } from 'lucide-react';
import { getPendingDeals, approveDeal, rejectDeal, getManagers } from '@/lib/api';
import { formatCurrency, formatDate } from '@/lib/utils';
import type { Deal, Manager } from '@/types/types';

export default function ApprovalsPage() {
  const [deals, setDeals]     = useState<Deal[]>([]);
  const [managers, setManagers] = useState<Manager[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const [pending, mgrs] = await Promise.all([getPendingDeals(), getManagers()]);
      setDeals(pending);
      setManagers(mgrs);
    } catch { toast.error('Ошибка загрузки'); }
    finally { setLoading(false); }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);

  function managerName(id: string) {
    return managers.find(m => m.id === id)?.name ?? '—';
  }

  async function handleApprove(id: string) {
    try {
      await approveDeal(id);
      toast.success('Сделка подтверждена');
      await load();
    } catch { toast.error('Ошибка'); }
  }

  async function handleReject(id: string) {
    try {
      await rejectDeal(id);
      toast.success('Сделка отклонена');
      await load();
    } catch { toast.error('Ошибка'); }
  }

  return (
    <AppLayout>
      <div className="space-y-6">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold">Подтверждение сделок</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground mt-0.5">
            Сделки, ожидающие вашего подтверждения
          </p>
        </div>

        {loading ? (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {[1, 2, 3].map(i => <div key={i} className="h-20 rounded-lg bg-muted animate-pulse" />)}
          </div>
        ) : deals.length === 0 ? (
          <Card>
            <CardContent className="py-16 text-center">
              <CheckCircle size={36} className="mx-auto mb-3 text-muted-foreground/40" />
              <p className="text-muted-foreground">Нет сделок, ожидающих подтверждения</p>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {deals.map(d => (
              <Card key={d.id} className="border border-border">
                <CardHeader className="pb-2 pt-4 px-4">
                  <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                    <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                      <CardTitle className="text-sm font-medium flex items-center gap-2">
                        <Clock size={14} className="shrink-0 text-amber-500" />
                        <span className="truncate">{managerName(d.manager_id)}</span>
                        <Badge variant="outline" className="text-xs border-amber-300 text-amber-600 shrink-0">
                          Ожидает
                        </Badge>
                      </CardTitle>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex gap-2 shrink-0">
                      <AlertDialog>
                        <AlertDialogTrigger asChild>
                          <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" className="h-7 text-xs text-destructive hover:text-destructive border border-destructive/30">
                            <XCircle size={13} className="mr-1" />Отклонить
                          </Button>
                        </AlertDialogTrigger>
                        <AlertDialogContent className="max-w-[calc(100%-2rem)] md:max-w-lg">
                          <AlertDialogHeader>
                            <AlertDialogTitle>Отклонить сделку?</AlertDialogTitle>
                            <AlertDialogDescription>
                              Сделка {d.client_name ? `«${d.client_name}»` : ''} на {formatCurrency(d.total_amount)} будет отклонена.
                            </AlertDialogDescription>
                          </AlertDialogHeader>
                          <AlertDialogFooter>
                            <AlertDialogCancel>Отмена</AlertDialogCancel>
                            <AlertDialogAction
                              className="bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground hover:bg-destructive/90"
                              onClick={() => handleReject(d.id)}
                            >Отклонить</AlertDialogAction>
                          </AlertDialogFooter>
                        </AlertDialogContent>
                      </AlertDialog>
                      <Button
                        size="sm"
                        className="h-7 text-xs"
                        onClick={() => handleApprove(d.id)}
                      >
                        <CheckCircle size={13} className="mr-1" />Подтвердить
                      </Button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </CardHeader>
                <CardContent className="px-4 pb-4">
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-sm">
                    <div>
                      <span className="text-muted-foreground text-xs">Дата</span>
                      <p>{formatDate(d.deal_date)}</p>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <span className="text-muted-foreground text-xs">Клиент</span>
                      <p className="truncate">{d.client_name || '—'}</p>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <span className="text-muted-foreground text-xs">Сумма</span>
                      <p className="font-medium">{formatCurrency(d.total_amount)}</p>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <span className="text-muted-foreground text-xs">Оплата</span>
                      <p>{d.payment_method}</p>
                    </div>
                    {d.door_model && (
                      <div className="md:col-span-2">
                        <span className="text-muted-foreground text-xs">Модель</span>
                        <p>{d.door_model}</p>
                      </div>
                    )}
                    {d.comment && (
                      <div className="md:col-span-2">
                        <span className="text-muted-foreground text-xs">Комментарий</span>
                        <p className="text-muted-foreground">{d.comment}</p>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </CardContent>
              </Card>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </AppLayout>
  );
}

// ─── Balance summary (transfers cancel out per channel) ────────────────────────
function BalanceSummary({ expenses, income, transfers }: {
  expenses: Expense[]; income: Income[]; transfers: Transfer[];
}) {
  const channels: string[] = ['Каспи', 'Халык', 'Фридом', 'Наличные'];
  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
      {channels.map(ch => {
        const inc = income.filter(i => i.channel === ch).reduce((s, i) => s + Number(i.total_amount), 0);
        const exp = expenses.filter(e => e.channel === ch).reduce((s, e) => s + Number(e.amount), 0);
        // transfers: outgoing reduces balance, incoming increases — net effect zero across all channels
        const trOut = transfers.filter(t => t.from_channel === ch).reduce((s, t) => s + Number(t.amount), 0);
        const trIn  = transfers.filter(t => t.to_channel   === ch).reduce((s, t) => s + Number(t.amount), 0);
        const balance = inc - exp - trOut + trIn;
        return (
          <div key={ch} className="rounded-md border border-border p-3">
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{ch}</p>
            <p className={`text-base font-semibold mt-1 ${balance < 0 ? 'text-destructive' : 'text-foreground'}`}>
              {formatCurrency(balance)}
            </p>
            <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1 flex flex-col gap-0.5">
              <span>↑ {formatCurrency(inc)}</span>
              <span>↓ {formatCurrency(exp)}</span>
              {(trOut > 0 || trIn > 0) && (
                <span className="text-muted-foreground/70">⇄ {trIn > 0 ? `+${formatCurrency(trIn)}` : ''}{trOut > 0 ? ` −${formatCurrency(trOut)}` : ''}</span>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        );
      })}

Old String: ```
import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import AppLayout from '@/components/layouts/AppLayout';
import {
  Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue,
} from '@/components/ui/select';
import { getManagers, getDeals, getSalesPlans, upsertSalesPlan } from '@/lib/api';
import { getCurrentMonthYear, getAvailableMonths, monthYearToLabel } from '@/lib/utils';
import type { Manager, Deal, SalesPlan } from '@/types/types';
import ManagerSalesSection from '@/components/sales/ManagerSalesSection';

export default function SalesPage() {
  const [monthYear, setMonthYear] = useState(getCurrentMonthYear());
  const [managers, setManagers] = useState<Manager[]>([]);
  const [deals, setDeals] = useState<Record<string, Deal[]>>({});
  const [plans, setPlans] = useState<SalesPlan[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const planSaveTimers = useRef<Record<string, ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>>({});

  const loadData = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const mgrs = await getManagers();
      setManagers(mgrs);
      const [allPlans, ...dealsByManager] = await Promise.all([
        getSalesPlans(monthYear),
        ...mgrs.map(m => getDeals(m.id, monthYear)),
      ]);
      setPlans(allPlans as SalesPlan[]);
      const dealsMap: Record<string, Deal[]> = {};
      mgrs.forEach((m, i) => { dealsMap[m.id] = dealsByManager[i] as Deal[]; });
      setDeals(dealsMap);
    } catch {
      // silent
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [monthYear]);

  useEffect(() => { loadData(); }, [loadData]);

  function getPlan(managerId: string): SalesPlan | undefined {
    return plans.find(p => p.manager_id === managerId && p.month_year === monthYear);
  }

  function handlePlanChange(managerId: string, field: 'plan_amount' | 'net_profit_plan' | 'dividends_plan', value: number) {
    setPlans(prev => {
      const existing = prev.find(p => p.manager_id === managerId && p.month_year === monthYear);
      if (existing) {
        return prev.map(p =>
          p.manager_id === managerId && p.month_year === monthYear
            ? { ...p, [field]: value }
            : p
        );
      }
      return [...prev, {
        id: `temp-${managerId}`,
        manager_id: managerId,
        month_year: monthYear,
        plan_amount: 0,
        net_profit_plan: null,
        dividends_plan: null,
        created_at: '',
        updated_at: '',
        [field]: value,
      }];
    });

    // Debounced save
    const key = `${managerId}:${monthYear}`;
    clearTimeout(planSaveTimers.current[key]);
    planSaveTimers.current[key] = setTimeout(async () => {
      const currentPlan = plans.find(p => p.manager_id === managerId && p.month_year === monthYear);
      const updated = { ...(currentPlan || { plan_amount: 0, net_profit_plan: null, dividends_plan: null }), [field]: value };
      await upsertSalesPlan({
        manager_id: managerId,
        month_year: monthYear,
        plan_amount: Number(updated.plan_amount) || 0,
        net_profit_plan: updated.net_profit_plan != null ? Number(updated.net_profit_plan) : null,
        dividends_plan: updated.dividends_plan != null ? Number(updated.dividends_plan) : null,
      });
    }, 800);
  }

  const months = getAvailableMonths();

  return (
    <AppLayout>
      <div className="space-y-6">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between gap-3">
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold">Продажи</h1>
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground mt-0.5">Учёт сделок по менеджерам</p>
          </div>
          <Select value={monthYear} onValueChange={setMonthYear}>
            <SelectTrigger className="w-52">
              <SelectValue />
            </SelectTrigger>
            <SelectContent>
              {months.map(m => (
                <SelectItem key={m} value={m}>{monthYearToLabel(m)}</SelectItem>
              ))}
            </SelectContent>
          </Select>
        </div>

        {loading ? (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            {[1, 2, 3].map(i => (
              <div key={i} className="h-32 rounded-lg bg-muted animate-pulse" />
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : managers.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-center py-16 text-muted-foreground">
            <p>Нет менеджеров.</p>
            <p className="text-sm mt-1">Перейдите в раздел «Менеджеры» и добавьте первого.</p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            {managers.map(m => (
              <ManagerSalesSection
                key={m.id}
                manager={m}
                monthYear={monthYear}
                deals={deals[m.id] || []}
                plan={getPlan(m.id)}
                onPlanChange={handlePlanChange}
                onRefresh={loadData}
              />
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </AppLayout>

New String: ```
import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import AppLayout from '@/components/layouts/AppLayout';
import {
  Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue,
} from '@/components/ui/select';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { useAuth } from '@/contexts/AuthContext';
import { getManagers, getDeals, getSalesPlans, upsertSalesPlan } from '@/lib/api';
import { getCurrentMonthYear, getAvailableMonths, monthYearToLabel } from '@/lib/utils';
import type { Manager, Deal, SalesPlan } from '@/types/types';
import { ROLES, SALES_PAGE_ROLES } from '@/types/types';
import ManagerSalesSection from '@/components/sales/ManagerSalesSection';

export default function SalesPage() {
  const { isAdmin, profile } = useAuth();
  const [monthYear, setMonthYear] = useState(getCurrentMonthYear());
  const [managers, setManagers]   = useState<Manager[]>([]);
  const [deals, setDeals]         = useState<Record<string, Deal[]>>({});
  const [plans, setPlans]         = useState<SalesPlan[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading]     = useState(true);

  // Filters (admin-only)
  const [roleFilter, setRoleFilter]         = useState<string>('all');
  const [employeeFilter, setEmployeeFilter] = useState<string>('all');

  const planSaveTimers = useRef<Record<string, ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>>({});

  const loadData = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const mgrs = await getManagers();
      setManagers(mgrs);
      const [allPlans, ...dealsByManager] = await Promise.all([
        getSalesPlans(monthYear),
        ...mgrs.map(m => getDeals(m.id, monthYear)),
      ]);
      setPlans(allPlans as SalesPlan[]);
      const dealsMap: Record<string, Deal[]> = {};
      mgrs.forEach((m, i) => { dealsMap[m.id] = dealsByManager[i] as Deal[]; });
      setDeals(dealsMap);
    } catch { /* silent */ }
    finally { setLoading(false); }
  }, [monthYear]);

  useEffect(() => { loadData(); }, [loadData]);

  function getPlan(managerId: string): SalesPlan | undefined {
    return plans.find(p => p.manager_id === managerId && p.month_year === monthYear);
  }

  function handlePlanChange(managerId: string, field: 'plan_amount' | 'net_profit_plan' | 'dividends_plan', value: number) {
    setPlans(prev => {
      const existing = prev.find(p => p.manager_id === managerId && p.month_year === monthYear);
      if (existing) return prev.map(p => p.manager_id === managerId && p.month_year === monthYear ? { ...p, [field]: value } : p);
      return [...prev, { id: `temp-${managerId}`, manager_id: managerId, month_year: monthYear, plan_amount: 0, net_profit_plan: null, dividends_plan: null, created_at: '', updated_at: '', [field]: value }];
    });
    const key = `${managerId}:${monthYear}`;
    clearTimeout(planSaveTimers.current[key]);
    planSaveTimers.current[key] = setTimeout(async () => {
      // Read from latest ref snapshot to avoid stale closure
      const currentPlan = plans.find(p => p.manager_id === managerId && p.month_year === monthYear);
      const updated = { ...(currentPlan || { plan_amount: 0, net_profit_plan: null, dividends_plan: null }), [field]: value };
      await upsertSalesPlan({
        manager_id: managerId,
        month_year: monthYear,
        plan_amount: Number(updated.plan_amount) || 0,
        net_profit_plan: updated.net_profit_plan != null ? Number(updated.net_profit_plan) : null,
        dividends_plan: updated.dividends_plan != null ? Number(updated.dividends_plan) : null,
      });
    }, 800);
  }

  // ── Filtering ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // Employees always see only their own manager section
  // Admins see all by default (filtered to sales roles), can switch role/employee
  const visibleManagers = managers.filter(m => {
    if (!isAdmin) {
      // employee sees only their own section
      return m.id === profile?.manager_id;
    }
    // admin: role filter
    if (roleFilter !== 'all' && m.role !== roleFilter) return false;
    // admin: employee filter
    if (employeeFilter !== 'all' && m.id !== employeeFilter) return false;
    // admin default: show only sales roles unless filter overridden
    if (roleFilter === 'all' && !SALES_PAGE_ROLES.includes(m.role)) return false;
    return true;
  });

  const months = getAvailableMonths();

  return (
    <AppLayout>
      <div className="space-y-5">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-start md:justify-between gap-3">
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold">Продажи</h1>
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground mt-0.5">Учёт сделок по сотрудникам</p>
          </div>
          <Select value={monthYear} onValueChange={setMonthYear}>
            <SelectTrigger className="w-52 shrink-0">
              <SelectValue />
            </SelectTrigger>
            <SelectContent>
              {months.map(m => <SelectItem key={m} value={m}>{monthYearToLabel(m)}</SelectItem>)}
            </SelectContent>
          </Select>
        </div>

        {/* Filters — admin only */}
        {isAdmin && (
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-center">
            {/* Role filter */}
            <Select value={roleFilter} onValueChange={v => { setRoleFilter(v); setEmployeeFilter('all'); }}>
              <SelectTrigger className="w-52 h-8 text-sm">
                <SelectValue placeholder="Должность" />
              </SelectTrigger>
              <SelectContent>
                <SelectItem value="all">Только менеджеры (по умолч.)</SelectItem>
                {ROLES.map(r => <SelectItem key={r} value={r}>{r}</SelectItem>)}
              </SelectContent>
            </Select>

            {/* Employee filter */}
            <Select value={employeeFilter} onValueChange={setEmployeeFilter}>
              <SelectTrigger className="w-48 h-8 text-sm">
                <SelectValue placeholder="Сотрудник" />
              </SelectTrigger>
              <SelectContent>
                <SelectItem value="all">Все</SelectItem>
                {managers
                  .filter(m => roleFilter === 'all' ? SALES_PAGE_ROLES.includes(m.role) : m.role === roleFilter)
                  .map(m => <SelectItem key={m.id} value={m.id}>{m.name}</SelectItem>)}
              </SelectContent>
            </Select>

            {(roleFilter !== 'all' || employeeFilter !== 'all') && (
              <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" className="h-8 text-xs"
                onClick={() => { setRoleFilter('all'); setEmployeeFilter('all'); }}>
                Сбросить
              </Button>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {loading ? (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            {[1, 2, 3].map(i => <div key={i} className="h-32 rounded-lg bg-muted animate-pulse" />)}
          </div>
        ) : visibleManagers.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-center py-16 text-muted-foreground">
            <p>Нет сотрудников для отображения.</p>
            {isAdmin && <p className="text-sm mt-1">Измените фильтр или добавьте сотрудников.</p>}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            {visibleManagers.map(m => (
              <ManagerSalesSection
                key={m.id}
                manager={m}
                monthYear={monthYear}
                deals={deals[m.id] || []}
                plan={getPlan(m.id)}
                onPlanChange={isAdmin ? handlePlanChange : undefined}
                onRefresh={loadData}
              />
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </AppLayout>
  );
}

    21 interface RowProps {

    23   setting: SalarySetting | undefined;
    24   deals: Deal[];
    25   onSaved: () => void;
    26 }
    27 
    28 function ManagerSalaryRow({ manager, setting, deals, onSaved }: RowProps) {
    29   const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);
    30   const [base, setBase] = useState(String(setting?.base_salary ?? 0));
    31   const [pct, setPct] = useState(String(setting?.commission_pct ?? 0));
    32   const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
    33 
    34   useEffect(() => {
    35     setBase(String(setting?.base_salary ?? 0));
    36     setPct(String(setting?.commission_pct ?? 0));
    37   }, [setting]);
    38

    40   const revenue = deals.reduce((s, d) => s + Number(d.salary_amount ?? d.total_amount), 0);
    41   const baseSalary = Number(base) || 0;
    42   const commPct = Number(pct) || 0;
    43   const commission = revenue * (commPct / 100);
    44   const total = baseSalary + commission;
    45 
    46   async function save() {
    47     if (baseSalary < 0 || commPct < 0) { toast.error('Значения не могут быть отрицательными'); return; }
    48     setSaving(true);
    49     try {
    50       await upsertSalarySetting({ manager_id: manager.id, base_salary: baseSalary, commission_pct: commPct });
    51       toast.success('Настройки сохранены');
    52       onSaved();
    53       setEditing(false);
    54     } catch { toast.error('Ошибка сохранения'); } finally { setSaving(false); }
    55   }
    56 
    57   function cancel() {
    58     setBase(String(setting?.base_salary ?? 0));
    59     setPct(String(setting?.commission_pct ?? 0));
    60     setEditing(false);
    61   }
    62 
    63   return (
    64     <tr className="border-b border-border last:border-0">

    80           />
    81         ) : formatCurrency(baseSalary)}
    82       </td>

    92           />
    93         ) : `${commPct}%`}
    94       </td>
    95

   110             <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" className="h-7 w-7 text-primary" onClick={save} disabled={saving}><Check size={13} /></Button>
   111             <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" className="h-7 w-7" onClick={cancel}><X size={13} /></Button>
   112           </div>
   113         ) : (
   114           <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" className="h-7 w-7" onClick={() => setEditing(true)}><Pencil size={13} /></Button>
   115         )}
   116       </td>
   117     </tr>
   118   );
   119 }

   121 // ─── Page ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   122 export default function SalaryPage() {
   123   const [managers, setManagers] = useState<Manager[]>([]);
   124   const [settings, setSettings] = useState<SalarySetting[]>([]);
   125   const [deals, setDeals] = useState<Deal[]>([]);

   136         getSalarySettings(),
   137         getAllDealsForMonth(monthYear),
   138       ]);

   140     } catch { /* silent */ } finally { setLoading(false); }
   141   }, [monthYear]);

   144 
   145   const totalSalary = managers.reduce((s, m) => {
   146     const sett = settings.find(ss => ss.manager_id === m.id);
   147     const rev = deals
   148       .filter(d => d.manager_id === m.id)
   149       .reduce((a, d) => a + Number(d.salary_amount ?? d.total_amount), 0);
   150     const base = Number(sett?.base_salary ?? 0);
   151     const pct = Number(sett?.commission_pct ?? 0);
   152     return s + base + rev * (pct / 100);
   153   }, 0);
   154 
   155   return (
   156     <AppLayout>
   157       <div className="space-y-6">
   158         {/* Header */}
   159         <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between gap-3">
   160           <div>
   161             <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold">Зарплаты</h1>
   162             <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground mt-0.5">Оклад + процент от продаж по менеджерам</p>
   163           </div>
   164           <Select value={monthYear} onValueChange={setMonthYear}>
   165             <SelectTrigger className="w-52">
   166               <SelectValue />
   167             </SelectTrigger>
   168             <SelectContent>
   169               {months.map(m => <SelectItem key={m} value={m}>{monthYearToLabel(m)}</SelectItem>)}
   170             </SelectContent>
   171           </Select>
   172         </div>
   173 
   174         {/* Summary card */}
   175         {!loading && (
   176           <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
   177             <div className="rounded-md border border-border p-3">
   178               <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Общий ФОТ ({monthYearToLabel(monthYear)})</p>
   179               <p className="text-base font-semibold mt-1">{formatCurrency(totalSalary)}</p>
   180             </div>
   181             <div className="rounded-md border border-border p-3">
   182               <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Менеджеров</p>
   183               <p className="text-base font-semibold mt-1">{managers.length}</p>
   184             </div>
   185             <div className="rounded-md border border-border p-3">
   186               <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Сделок за месяц</p>
   187               <p className="text-base font-semibold mt-1">{deals.length}</p>
   188             </div>
   189           </div>
   190         )}
   191 
   192         {/* Table */}
   193         <Card className="border border-border">
   194           <CardHeader className="pb-3">
   195             <CardTitle className="text-base">Расчёт зарплат — {monthYearToLabel(monthYear)}</CardTitle>
   196             <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-0.5">
   197               Нажмите <Pencil size={10} className="inline mb-0.5" /> чтобы изменить оклад или процент
   198             </p>
   199           </CardHeader>
   200           <CardContent>
   201             {loading ? (
   202               <div className="space-y-2">
   203                 {[1, 2, 3].map(i => <div key={i} className="h-10 bg-muted rounded animate-pulse" />)}
   204               </div>
   205             ) : managers.length === 0 ? (
   206               <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground text-center py-8">Нет менеджеров</p>
   207             ) : (
   208               <div className="w-full overflow-x-auto">
   209                 <table className="w-full text-sm">
   210                   <thead>
   211                     <tr className="border-b border-border">
   212                       <th className="text-left whitespace-nowrap py-2 pr-4 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Менеджер</th>
   213                       <th className="text-right whitespace-nowrap py-2 px-3 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Сделок</th>
   214                       <th className="text-right whitespace-nowrap py-2 px-3 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Выручка</th>
   215                       <th className="text-right whitespace-nowrap py-2 px-3 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Оклад (₸)</th>
   216                       <th className="text-right whitespace-nowrap py-2 px-3 font-medium text-muted-foreground">% комиссии</th>
   217                       <th className="text-right whitespace-nowrap py-2 px-3 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Комиссия (₸)</th>
   218                       <th className="text-right whitespace-nowrap py-2 pl-3 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Итого ЗП</th>
   219                       <th className="whitespace-nowrap py-2 pl-3 w-16"></th>
   220                     </tr>
   221                   </thead>
   222                   <tbody>
   223                     {managers.map(m => (
   224                       <ManagerSalaryRow
   225                         key={m.id}
   226                         manager={m}
   227                         setting={settings.find(s => s.manager_id === m.id)}
   228                         deals={deals.filter(d => d.manager_id === m.id)}
   229                         onSaved={load}
   230                       />
   231                     ))}
   232                     {/* Totals */}
   233                     <tr className="border-t border-border bg-muted/30">
   234                       <td className="py-2.5 pr-4 text-sm font-semibold" colSpan={6}>ИТОГО ФОТ</td>
   235                       <td className="py-2.5 pl-3 text-right text-sm font-semibold tabular-nums">{formatCurrency(totalSalary)}</td>
   236                       <td></td>
   237                     </tr>
   238                   </tbody>
   239                 </table>
   240               </div>
   241             )}
   242           </CardContent>
   243         </Card>
   244 
   245         {/* Legend */}
   246         <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
   247           Формула: <span className="font-medium">Итого ЗП = Оклад + Выручка × % комиссии</span>.
   248           Выручка берётся из сделок за выбранный месяц. Изменения оклада и процента применяются сразу ко всем месяцам.
   249         </p>
   250       </div>
   251     </AppLayout>
   252   );
   253 }

    31 function buildChartData(deals: Deal[], managers: Manager[], months: string[]): MonthRow[] {

    11 } from '@/components/ui/alert-dialog';
    12 import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogFooter, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from '@/components/ui/dialog';
    13 import { toast } from 'sonner';
    14 import { Plus, Pencil, Trash2, Users } from 'lucide-react';
    15 import { getManagers, createManager, updateManager, deleteManager } from '@/lib/api';
    16 import type { Manager } from '@/types/types';
    17 
    18 const ROLE_PRESETS = ['Менеджер', 'Бухгалтер', 'Директор', 'Кладовщик', 'Курьер'];
    19 
    20 export default function ManagersPage() {
    21   const [managers, setManagers] = useState<Manager[]>([]);
    22   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    23   const [newName, setNewName] = useState('');
    24   const [newRole, setNewRole] = useState('Менеджер');
    25   const [editManager, setEditManager] = useState<Manager | null>(null);
    26   const [editName, setEditName] = useState('');
    27   const [editRole, setEditRole] = useState('Менеджер');
    28   const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
    29 
    30   const load = useCallback(async () => {
    31     setLoading(true);
    32     try {
    33       setManagers(await getManagers());
    34     } catch {
    35       toast.error('Ошибка загрузки');
    36     } finally {
    37       setLoading(false);
    38     }
    39   }, []);
    40 
    41   useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);
    42 
    43   async function handleCreate() {
    44     const name = newName.trim();
    45     if (!name) { toast.error('Введите имя сотрудника'); return; }
    46     setSaving(true);
    47     try {
    48       await createManager(name, newRole || 'Менеджер');
    49       setNewName(''); setNewRole('Менеджер');
    50       toast.success(`Сотрудник «${name}» добавлен`);
    51       await load();
    52     } catch {
    53       toast.error('Не удалось добавить');
    54     } finally {
    55       setSaving(false);
    56     }
    57   }
    58 
    59   async function handleUpdate() {
    60     if (!editManager) return;
    61     const name = editName.trim();
    62     if (!name) { toast.error('Введите имя'); return; }
    63     setSaving(true);
    64     try {
    65       await updateManager(editManager.id, name, editRole || 'Менеджер');
    66       toast.success('Данные обновлены');
    67       setEditManager(null);
    68       await load();
    69     } catch {
    70       toast.error('Не удалось обновить');
    71     } finally {
    72       setSaving(false);
    73     }
    74   }
    75 
    76   async function handleDelete(id: string, name: string) {
    77     try {
    78       await deleteManager(id);
    79       toast.success(`«${name}» удалён`);
    80       await load();
    81     } catch {
    82       toast.error('Не удалось удалить');
    83     }
    84   }
    85 
    86   return (
    87     <AppLayout>
    88       <div className="max-w-xl mx-auto space-y-6">
    89         <div>
    90           <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold text-foreground">Сотрудники</h1>
    91           <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground mt-1">Менеджеры и другие сотрудники</p>
    92         </div>
    93 
    94         {/* Add */}
    95         <Card>
    96           <CardHeader className="pb-3">
    97             <CardTitle className="text-base">Добавить сотрудника</CardTitle>
    98           </CardHeader>
    99           <CardContent className="space-y-3">
   100             <div className="space-y-1">
   101               <Label>Имя *</Label>
   102               <Input
   103                 placeholder="Имя сотрудника"
   104                 value={newName}
   105                 onChange={e => setNewName(e.target.value)}
   106                 onKeyDown={e => e.key === 'Enter' && handleCreate()}
   107               />
   108             </div>
   109             <div className="space-y-1">
   110               <Label>Должность</Label>
   111               <Input
   112                 placeholder="Менеджер"
   113                 value={newRole}
   114                 onChange={e => setNewRole(e.target.value)}
   115                 list="role-presets"
   116               />
   117               <datalist id="role-presets">
   118                 {ROLE_PRESETS.map(r => <option key={r} value={r} />)}
   119               </datalist>
   120             </div>
   121             <Button onClick={handleCreate} disabled={saving || !newName.trim()} className="w-full">
   122               <Plus size={16} className="mr-1" />Добавить
   123             </Button>
   124           </CardContent>
   125         </Card>
   126 
   127         {/* List */}
   128         <Card>
   129           <CardHeader className="pb-3">
   130             <CardTitle className="text-base flex items-center gap-2">
   131               <Users size={16} />
   132               Список сотрудников
   133               <span className="text-muted-foreground font-normal text-sm">({managers.length})</span>
   134             </CardTitle>
   135           </CardHeader>
   136           <CardContent>
   137             {loading ? (
   138               <div className="space-y-2">
   139                 {[1, 2, 3].map(i => <div key={i} className="h-10 rounded bg-muted animate-pulse" />)}
   140               </div>
   141             ) : managers.length === 0 ? (
   142               <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground text-center py-6">Нет сотрудников. Добавьте первого выше.</p>
   143             ) : (
   144               <ul className="divide-y divide-border">
   145                 {managers.map(m => (
   146                   <li key={m.id} className="flex items-center justify-between py-2.5 gap-2">
   147                     <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
   148                       <p className="text-sm font-medium text-foreground truncate">{m.name}</p>
   149                       <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{m.role || 'Менеджер'}</p>
   150                     </div>
   151                     <div className="flex gap-1 shrink-0">
   152                       <Button
   153                         variant="ghost" size="icon" className="h-8 w-8"
   154                         onClick={() => { setEditManager(m); setEditName(m.name); setEditRole(m.role || 'Менеджер'); }}
   155                       >
   156                         <Pencil size={14} />
   157                       </Button>
   158                       <AlertDialog>
   159                         <AlertDialogTrigger asChild>
   160                           <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" className="h-8 w-8 text-destructive hover:text-destructive">
   161                             <Trash2 size={14} />
   162                           </Button>
   163                         </AlertDialogTrigger>
   164                         <AlertDialogContent className="max-w-[calc(100%-2rem)] md:max-w-lg">
   165                           <AlertDialogHeader>
   166                             <AlertDialogTitle>Удалить «{m.name}»?</AlertDialogTitle>
   167                             <AlertDialogDescription>
   168                               Будут удалены все сделки, поступления и планы этого сотрудника. Действие необратимо.
   169                             </AlertDialogDescription>
   170                           </AlertDialogHeader>
   171                           <AlertDialogFooter>
   172                             <AlertDialogCancel>Отмена</AlertDialogCancel>
   173                             <AlertDialogAction
   174                               className="bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground hover:bg-destructive/90"
   175                               onClick={() => handleDelete(m.id, m.name)}
   176                             >Удалить</AlertDialogAction>
   177                           </AlertDialogFooter>
   178                         </AlertDialogContent>
   179                       </AlertDialog>
   180                     </div>
   181                   </li>
   182                 ))}
   183               </ul>
   184             )}
   185           </CardContent>
   186         </Card>
   187       </div>
   188 
   189       {/* Edit dialog */}
   190       <Dialog open={!!editManager} onOpenChange={v => !v && setEditManager(null)}>
   191         <DialogContent className="max-w-[calc(100%-2rem)] md:max-w-lg">
   192           <DialogHeader>
   193             <DialogTitle>Редактировать сотрудника</DialogTitle>
   194           </DialogHeader>
   195           <div className="space-y-3 py-1">
   196             <div className="space-y-1">
   197               <Label>Имя *</Label>
   198               <Input
   199                 value={editName}
   200                 onChange={e => setEditName(e.target.value)}
   201                 onKeyDown={e => e.key === 'Enter' && handleUpdate()}
   202                 placeholder="Имя"
   203               />
   204             </div>
   205             <div className="space-y-1">
   206               <Label>Должность</Label>
   207               <Input
   208                 value={editRole}
   209                 onChange={e => setEditRole(e.target.value)}
   210                 placeholder="Менеджер"
   211                 list="role-presets-edit"
   212               />
   213               <datalist id="role-presets-edit">
   214                 {ROLE_PRESETS.map(r => <option key={r} value={r} />)}
   215               </datalist>
   216             </div>
   217           </div>
   218           <DialogFooter>
   219             <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => setEditManager(null)}>Отмена</Button>
   220             <Button onClick={handleUpdate} disabled={saving || !editName.trim()}>Сохранить</Button>
   221           </DialogFooter>
   222         </DialogContent>
   223       </Dialog>
   224     </AppLayout>
   225   );
   226 }
   227 
   228 


</observation>
